Implementing would involve an OAuth device/loopback flow with tokens in ProfileVault, a Responses SSE client, and schema/usage normalization feeding the existing agent tool loop. |
| [Gallery pin and archive flags](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/gallery_flags.py): per-directory .flags.json store holding pinned_at, archived and manual-order keys beside media, failing safe to no flags on corruption | [gallery_flags.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/gallery_flags.py) | [AppModel+ImageGallery.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+ImageGallery.swift) | Missing | TS's image gallery (carousel of saved artifacts) is recency-ordered with trash as the only curation action; no pin, archive or drag order exists. | [Copy gallery media into project](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/gallery_projects.py): copies a generated image or clip into a chat project's sandbox media folder so the project's agent tools can use it, with symlink-safe directory walking | [gallery_projects.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/gallery_projects.py) | [AppModel+ImageGallery.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+ImageGallery.swift) | Partial | TS keeps generated images as per-profile artifacts attached to chats and has project organization plus agent file tools, but there is no flow that copies an artifact into a project's agent-visible folder. Improvement: a copy-into-project action wired to the agent workspace scope. |
| [Disk-backed image gallery](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/image_gallery.py): persists each generated image as a PNG with the full generation recipe embedded in text chunks (plus an A1111 parameters string) so downloaded files carry their own settings | [image_gallery.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/image_gallery.py) | [AppModel+ImageGallery.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+ImageGallery.swift) | Partial | TS persists generated images per profile under image-artifacts with ManagedAssetStore reference counting and deletion reconciliation, but the saved PNG does not embed the prompt, seed, size or steps, so an exported image loses its recipe. Improvement: write the request envelope into PNG metadata at save time. |
| [Media model auto-switch](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_auto_switch.py): resolves the model name on an image/video API request against downloaded media models and runs the picker's load, skipping when the resident build matches and proving downloads first | [media_auto_switch.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_auto_switch.py), [media_model_index.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_model_index.py), [media_locality.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_locality.py) | [ImageModelControls.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageModelControls.swift) | Partial | TS selects an image model manually in the Images workspace, creates its session on demand and caches it by path (so an identical pick skips a reload), but nothing resolves a request's model name, verifies download completeness by bytes, or swaps a text model out to make room. Improvement: request-driven resolution plus a memory-aware swap against the text session. |
| [Switch serialization and drain](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_switch_backends.py): waits out cross-owner GPU work a media switch would interrupt, serializes one switch per backend with cross-backend queueing, and answers impossible switches with retry-shaped refusals | [media_switch_backends.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_switch_backends.py), [media_switch_locks.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_switch_locks.py), [media_switch_errors.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/media_switch_errors.py) | none | Missing | TS has no API-driven model switching, so there are no races to drain or serialize; a manual model change is guarded by generating/submitting/opening states and deleteModel refusals. This becomes relevant only together with request-driven switching. |
| [Import compatibility stubs](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/_torchao_stub.py): stubs torchao/xformers imports that crash on Windows ROCm before transformers/diffusers load, and recovers the real unsloth packages from a namespace-package shadow on sys.path | [_torchao_stub.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/_torchao_stub.py), [import_guards.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/import_guards.py) | none | Missing | TS has no Python dependency graph to guard; its engine is statically linked via FFI, so the entire class of import-environment repair does not apply. Nothing to implement. |

## B6. Inference core part 3: audio and STT

Scope: dictation/STT engines and sidecars, neural TTS with audio codecs, native TTS and music
models, and the TTS clip gallery.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Dictation lifecycle (STT)](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_registry.py): one registry loads a dictation model on one of three engines (Transformers, whisper.cpp GGUF, llama.cpp mtmd), keeps it warm between dictations, and releases idle engines so only one is resident | [stt_registry.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_registry.py), [stt_sidecar.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_sidecar.py), [stt_transformers_worker.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_transformers_worker.py) | [PromptComposerControls.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/PromptComposerControls.swift) | Partial | The prompt mic button only forwards to macOS system dictation via NSApp startDictation, so there is no in-app STT model, no offline operation, and no model or engine choice, and it silently does nothing if macOS dictation is disabled. Implementing would involve a local Whisper runtime (whisper.cpp sidecar plus a curated GGML checkpoint download) feeding text into the prompt editor. |
| [whisper.cpp GGML sidecar](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_ggml_sidecar.py): serves curated unsloth whisper GGUF checkpoints through a whisper-server subprocess on a localhost port, about 2.5x faster than the Transformers path on Apple Silicon and CPU with f16 weights and warm keep-alive | [stt_ggml_sidecar.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_ggml_sidecar.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve shipping or downloading a whisper-server binary and single-file GGML checkpoints, with a localhost HTTP loop and idle unload; TurboSpark already runs its engine out of process for the daemon so the lifecycle pattern exists to copy. |
| [llama.cpp multimodal ASR sidecar](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_mtmd_sidecar.py): serves newer ASR models that whisper.cpp cannot load through llama-server with an audio mmproj (text model plus audio projector), driven by OpenAI audio content parts on an ephemeral localhost port | [stt_mtmd_sidecar.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_mtmd_sidecar.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve pairing two GGUF files per model (text model and audio mmproj) through the same subprocess management TurboSpark uses for chat, plus the shared keep-alive and cancel plumbing. |
| [Cancellable dictation model downloads](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_download_worker.py): runs each dictation model download in a spawned child process so cancel is a SIGTERM with byte-exact HF resume, and the HF token travels in the environment rather than argv | [stt_download_worker.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/stt_download_worker.py) | [ModelDownload.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ModelDownload.swift) | Missing | TurboSpark already has background model downloads with a transfer meter (ModelDownload, AppModel+Downloads), so the gap is wiring STT checkpoints into that surface with cancel-and-resume semantics rather than new infrastructure. |
| [Local TTS generation (Speak)](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/audio_codecs.py): generates speech WAV through neural audio codecs (SNAC for Orpheus, CSM for Sesame, BiCodec for Spark, DAC for OuteTTS) with auto/cpu/gpu device placement and client-safe error codes for cancel and unsupported backends | [audio_codecs.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/audio_codecs.py), [audio_device.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/audio_device.py), [audio_errors.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/audio_errors.py) | [AppSpeechSynthesizer.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Presentation/AppSpeechSynthesizer.swift), [MessageActionBarView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/MessageActionBarView.swift) | Partial | TurboSpark read-aloud uses AVSpeechSynthesizer OS voices only (surfaced in MessageActionBarView and OutputPaneView), so there is no model TTS, no voice model choice, and no generated audio artifact; a concrete improvement is adding one local TTS family with codec decode while keeping the OS synthesizer as the zero-download fallback, honoring a cpu/gpu placement preference like UNSLOTH_AUDIO_DEVICE. |
| [Native TTS and music backends](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/native_audio.py): runs Transformers-native audio models (Higgs TTS 2/3, Moss TTS local and nano, MiniMax Music 3) including music generation, gated behind version and remote-code checks before any torch import | [native_audio.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/native_audio.py) | none | Missing | Music generation has no analogue anywhere in the Swift app; implementing would involve a native-model registry, remote-code safety gates, and a generation route distinct from read-aloud. |
| [TTS clip gallery](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/audio_gallery.py): persists each generated clip as a WAV plus a JSON recipe sidecar under the workspace with atomic staged writes, size and count caps, pinning, reordering, and paginated listing | [audio_gallery.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/audio_gallery.py) | [AppModel+ImageGallery.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+ImageGallery.swift) | Missing | TurboSpark persists generated images only (AppModel+ImageGallery, Images views); implementing would involve an audio artifact store keyed like the image gallery plus playback and export UI. |

## B7. Inference core part 4: diffusion and image generation

Scope: the diffusion backend and its per-family, memory, quantization, speed, cache, and sd.cpp
fallback files, compared against TurboSpark's native Metal Z-Image-Turbo pipeline (crates/image)
and its Swift Images surface. Crate files are cited relative to the repo root.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Core diffusion backend](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion.py): torch singleton that loads a GGUF, safetensors, or full diffusers pipeline with background load progress and one live model | [diffusion.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion.py), [diffusion_families.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_families.py) | [ImageComposerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageComposerView.swift) | Partial | TS loads one packed .gturbo or MLX safetensors install at a time with progress, generate, and unload; no multi-format GGUF/fp8/diffusers pipeline assembly around a single-file transformer. |
| [Family-specific pipeline assembly](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_hidream.py): per-family shims that assemble HiDream, Ideogram 4, and Krea 2 pipelines and preflight FLUX.2 GGUF vs base compatibility | [diffusion_hidream.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_hidream.py), [diffusion_ideogram4.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_ideogram4.py), [diffusion_krea2.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_krea2.py), [diffusion_compat.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_compat.py) | [ImageModelControls.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageModelControls.swift) | Missing | TS serves one family, Z-Image-Turbo, whose variants are quantization levels of the same checkpoint; no FLUX, Qwen-Image, HiDream, Ideogram, or Krea support. |
| [Engine router and device policy](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_engine_router.py): picks diffusers vs native sd.cpp per device with env overrides, MPS gating, and fallback recording, plus CUDA/ROCm/XPU/MPS device and dtype mapping | [diffusion_engine_router.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_engine_router.py), [diffusion_device.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_device.py) | [AppModel+ImageGeneration.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+ImageGeneration.swift) | Partial | TS picks between its native Metal session and an MLX session per install, but the choice is by model provenance, not a hardware policy layer with env knobs or fallback reasons; macOS Metal is the only target. |
| [Native sd.cpp engine](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/sd_cpp_engine.py): stable-diffusion.cpp subprocess tier with pure argv building, per-family flags, lazy binary install, and shared GGUF assets for CPU or MPS hosts | [sd_cpp_engine.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/sd_cpp_engine.py), [sd_cpp_args.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/sd_cpp_args.py), [sd_cpp_backend.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/sd_cpp_backend.py) | [runtime.rs](../crates/image/src/runtime.rs) | Missing | TS instead runs its own native Metal engine for packed installs; there is no sd.cpp CPU fallback tier or lazy binary installer. |
| [Persistent sd-server manager](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/sd_cpp_server.py): keeps one sd-server process loaded across many generations over HTTP instead of reloading multi-GB GGUF per image | [sd_cpp_server.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/sd_cpp_server.py) | [MLXImageGenerationSession.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/MLXImageGenerationSession.swift) | Partial | TS keeps a resident image session (native FFI session or MLX pipeline) until explicit unload, so weights are not reloaded per image; there is no subprocess or HTTP server manager. |
| [LoRA support](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_lora.py): curated plus local adapter catalog, id-based resolution with HF download, prompt-tag selection for the native engine, and a supports_lora gate | [diffusion_lora.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_lora.py) | [ImageGenerationSettings.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageGenerationSettings.swift) | Missing | Settings expose only image count, fixed size, and seed; no adapter picker, weights, or prompt tags anywhere in the Swift image UI. |
| [ControlNet support](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_controlnet.py): family-gated ControlNet discovery, repo resolution, control-image preprocessing with a dependency-free canny map, and a capability gate | [diffusion_controlnet.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_controlnet.py) | [ImageComposerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageComposerView.swift) | Missing | The composer is prompt-only text to image; there is no control-image input, picker, or preprocessing. |
| [Batched multi-image planning](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_batched.py): turns prompt lists, seed lists, or batch_size into per-image jobs with per-forward batching, chunking, and OOM halving | [diffusion_batched.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_batched.py) | [ImageGenerationSequence.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ImageGenerationSequence.swift) | Partial | TS offers 1 to 4 images as sequential batch-one requests with seed advanced by offset, the same seed-derivation convention, but no true batched forward, prompt lists, or OOM chunk backoff. |
| [Conditioning and edit workflows](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_conditioning.py): condition images and output geometry for reference and unified-edit flows, with annotate, paint, and mask conventions for localized Qwen-Image-2.1 editing | [diffusion_conditioning.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_conditioning.py) | [ImageComposerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageComposerView.swift) | Missing | No reference or source image input of any kind; generation is unconditional text to image with guidance fixed at zero. |
| [Prompt-conditioning caches](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_prompt_cache.py): host-RAM LRU of text-encoder outputs plus an opt-in persistent on-disk conditioning cache keyed by prompt hash and family | [diffusion_prompt_cache.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_prompt_cache.py), [diffusion_cond_cache.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_cond_cache.py) | [conditioning.rs](../crates/image/src/conditioning.rs) | Missing | TS re-runs Qwen3 conditioning for every job; there is no embedding cache, LRU, or persistent store. |

| [Compile and speed tiers](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_speed.py): off/default/eager/max speed ladder with torch.compile, prewarmed compile mega-cache, compiled GGUF dequant, dynamic prompt-length shaping, and a persistent cuDNN render thread | [diffusion_speed.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_speed.py), [diffusion_compile_cache.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_compile_cache.py), [diffusion_gguf_compile.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_gguf_compile.py), [diffusion_dynamic_text.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_dynamic_text.py), [diffusion_render_thread.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_render_thread.py) | [metal.rs](../crates/image/src/metal.rs) | Missing | TS dispatches fixed hand-written Metal kernels; IG5 is intentionally open for measured optimization, but there is no compile ladder or portable cache artifact. |
| [CUDA graph capture](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_cuda_graph.py): static-buffer graph capture of one denoiser forward with capture-safe forwards, prefix-KV compaction hooks, int_mm padding, and a torchao sync-removal patch | [diffusion_cuda_graph.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_cuda_graph.py), [diffusion_capture_safe.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_capture_safe.py), [diffusion_prefix_kv.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_prefix_kv.py), [diffusion_quant_pad.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_quant_pad.py), [diffusion_torchao_patches.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/diffusion_torchao_patches.py) | [metal_ops.rs](../crates/image/src/metal_ops.rs) | Missing | This machinery is CUDA-specific; the Metal path has no graph-capture equivalent exposed in the app. |

## B8. Inference core part 5: video

Scope: the video generation backend, family support, frame export and NVENC, galleries, and the
per-family pipeline assembly files.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Text-to-video generation](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video.py): local diffusers video backend for Wan 2.2, HunyuanVideo 1.5, LTX-2, and MiniMax-H3 with frame/fps control, per-generation cancel, frames-aware memory planning with always-on VAE tiling, and MP4 H.264 export from trusted repos only | [video.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video.py), [video_families.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_families.py) | none | Missing | The word video does not appear anywhere in the Swift app tree; implementing would involve a video DiT runtime with family routing separate from the image pipeline (TurboSpark image generation is MLX/Metal based), a frame-lattice shape gate, and heavy VRAM planning on Metal. |
| [MP4 export pipeline](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_frames.py): converts decoded float frames to uint8 RGB on-device with pinned-memory copies (bit-identical to the reference path) and offers opt-in NVENC H.264 encoding with libx264 fallback | [video_frames.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_frames.py), [video_nvenc.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_nvenc.py) | none | Missing | On Apple Silicon the analogue would be VideoToolbox hardware encode instead of NVENC; implementing would involve frame staging into CVPixelBuffers and a codec fallback chain. |
| [Video gallery](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_gallery.py): stores each result as an MP4 plus a JSON recipe sidecar with job records for in-flight generations, first-frame WebP thumbnails, WebM and GIF transcode, flags, reordering, and delete/clear | [video_gallery.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_gallery.py) | [AppModel+ImageGallery.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+ImageGallery.swift) | Missing | TurboSpark has an image gallery carousel and cards (Images views) whose persistence and job-state pattern (AppImageJob) could be extended to video artifacts with thumbnail extraction via AVAssetImageGenerator. |
| [LTX-2.3 pipeline with synchronized audio](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_ltx2.py): assembles the full LTX-2.3 stack (transformer from single-file GGUF with key renames, connectors, video and audio VAEs, vocoder, Gemma3 text encoder) so generated clips carry a muxed audio track | [video_ltx2.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_ltx2.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve the same manual component assembly to work around loader gaps, plus audio-video muxing on export; high complexity and model size make it a late-stage addition. |
| [MiniMax-H3 model support](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_minimax_h3.py): shared repo and VRAM-floor constants plus two loaders that reshape hosted pre-quantized H3 assets (curve-form pruned AdaLN denoiser and a layer-pruned ConvRot INT8 Qwen3-VL conditioner) so they load into diffusers | [video_minimax_h3.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_minimax_h3.py), [video_minimax_h3_adaln.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_minimax_h3_adaln.py), [video_minimax_h3_te.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_minimax_h3_te.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve checkpoint-shaping code of this kind only after a base video backend exists; without it the hosted H3 artifacts are unloadable, which is why Unsloth carries this layer. |
| [H3 VAE acceleration](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_minimax_h3_vae.py): speeds up H3 video VAE encode and decode with Triton-fused GroupNorm/SiLU/rope kernels and tile batching plus an opt-in int8 decoder, NVIDIA-only with automatic stock fallback | [video_minimax_h3_vae.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_minimax_h3_vae.py) | none | Missing | A Metal port would mean fusing the same norm/activation chain as Metal kernels behind the existing gpu crate kernel dispatch, with env-style levers replaced by settings toggles. |
| [Pre-quantized video denoisers](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_denoiser_prequant.py): seeds a conventional video pipeline with hosted pre-quantized denoiser weights, all-or-nothing per component, with task-specific handling so the MoE second expert never loads expert 1's file | [video_denoiser_prequant.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/inference/video_denoiser_prequant.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve a pre-quant checkpoint registry keyed by family and quant scheme, mirroring how TurboSpark model-io already handles quantized chat weights but extended to diffusion components. |

## B9. Training core

Scope: `studio/backend/core/training` (LLM and diffusion LoRA trainers, job orchestration,
datasets, provenance), `core/data_recipe` (synthetic data recipes), `core/export` (checkpoint
export), `core/library`, and the training/recipe/dataset/export API schemas in
`studio/backend/models`. TurboSpark has no training surface, so most rows are Missing; Partial
rows mark reusable job/download/export patterns.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [LLM fine-tuning engine](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/trainer.py): loads base models in 4/16-bit, attaches LoRA/QLoRA adapters, preprocesses text, VLM and audio datasets, and drives the Unsloth SFT loop with progress callbacks | [trainer.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/trainer.py), [__init__.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/__init__.py), [fsdp2_design_notes.md](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/fsdp2_design_notes.md) | none | Missing | TS has no training path at all (grep for train/lora/finetune in the Swift tree only hits "Trained Context" labels and offline permission-gate model references); implementing it would mean a Metal-backed or Python-subprocess trainer crate plus a Swift job UI, a far larger lift than the inference engine itself. |
| [Training job orchestrator](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/training.py): runs each fine-tune in a fresh spawn subprocess, pumps worker events, keeps per-run SQLite rows and matplotlib metric plots, and enforces one run at a time | [training.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/training.py), [worker.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/worker.py), [lifecycle.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/lifecycle.py) | [ModelDownload.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ModelDownload.swift), [ModelDownloadHistoryStore.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ModelDownloadHistoryStore.swift) | Partial | TS has a real job lifecycle analog (download request enum, retry, vault-persisted history) but nothing for training runs; a concrete improvement is reusing the ModelDownload state machine and history-store pattern for persisted training run records with cancel/retry. |
| [Account-scoped job boundaries](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/account_jobs.py): ownership and execution guards shared by all long-running Studio jobs so subprocesses inherit the right account and cannot touch foreign paths | [account_jobs.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/account_jobs.py) | none | Missing | TS jobs are single-user local downloads with no account multiplexing; implementing per-profile job ownership would involve extending the profile vault's job records with owner identity and path validation. |
| [max_steps row bounding](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/dataset_bounds.py): computes the row bound for short runs so a 30-step job does not tokenize millions of rows, torch-free for CPU-only hosts | [dataset_bounds.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/dataset_bounds.py) | none | Missing | Purely a training-side optimization; would only matter once a trainer exists, where the same pre-count trick applies before dataset preparation. |
| [Diffusion LoRA trainers](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_lora_trainer.py): image and video LoRA training loops for SDXL, flow-matching DiT families (FLUX, Qwen-Image, LTX-2) and joint video+audio MiniMax-H3, with shared config, events, stop protocol and family registry | [diffusion_lora_trainer.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_lora_trainer.py), [diffusion_dit_trainer.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_dit_trainer.py), [diffusion_h3_trainer.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_h3_trainer.py), [diffusion_train_common.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_train_common.py) | none | Missing | TS ships inference-only image generation (MLXImageGenerationSession.swift) with zero adapter training; this entire modality would need a new trainer family, and per AGENTS.md any such claim needs real Metal hardware gates. |
| [Diffusion training extras](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_train_extras.py): opt-in LoRA EMA with a second exported adapter, persistent conditioning cache, and aspect-ratio bucketing | [diffusion_train_extras.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_train_extras.py) | none | Missing | Quality accessories for a trainer TS does not have; implementation is deferred until a base diffusion trainer exists and would follow the same opt-in config plumbing. |
| [Diffusion resumable checkpoints](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_checkpoint.py): single writer/reader of optimizer, scheduler, EMA shadow and step state under checkpoint-N dirs so a stopped run continues instead of restarting | [diffusion_checkpoint.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_checkpoint.py), [resume.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/resume.py) | none | Missing | resume.py validates resumable outputs and guards against foreign paths; TS's nearest analog is download resume in ModelDownload, but checkpoint resume requires trainer state serialization that does not exist anywhere in TS. |
| [Diffusion training job service](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_training_service.py): small job service spawning the trainer subprocess, pumping model_load/progress/complete/error events into a status snapshot, with injectable context for tests | [diffusion_training_service.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_training_service.py) | [DownloadManagerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Components/DownloadManagerView.swift) | Partial | TS's download manager already snapshots active work and history across launches, which is the same shape minus subprocess event pumping; the gap is any training subprocess to pump, so this stays a pattern to copy rather than a feature to port. |
| [Diffusion dataset layer](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_clip_formats.py): dataset discovery, upload allowlists, caption management and the shared video-container extension list, plus the MiniMax-H3 clip geometry (frames, audio rows, canvas rule) | [diffusion_clip_formats.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_clip_formats.py), [diffusion_h3_clips.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/diffusion_h3_clips.py) | none | Missing | TS has no dataset concept in the app at all; a minimal version would be a media import/pairing surface in Swift plus caption editing, but only if training arrives first. |
| [Dataset source loaders](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/s3_dataset.py): S3/parquet/jsonl/csv download into the local dataset path, eval-split detection with minimum row thresholds, and pre-counted row bounds | [s3_dataset.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/s3_dataset.py), [eval_dataset.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/eval_dataset.py) | none | Missing | TS downloads model weights from HF Hub (ranged_download in repack) but has no dataset ingestion; implementing it would mean a dataset import surface plus schema sniffing in Swift or a helper crate. |
| [Resource provenance tracking](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/provenance.py): records which hub repo, URL or local path each dataset and model came from, versioned, so runs stay traceable to their inputs | [provenance.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/training/provenance.py) | [ModelDownloadHistoryStore.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ModelDownloadHistoryStore.swift) | Partial | TS keeps download source repos in the vault history (private-repo aware, capped at 12) which is provenance for models only; the gap is datasets and training inputs, and the improvement is generalizing the history store into a typed provenance record. |
| [Synthetic data recipe engine](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/service.py): NeMo DataDesigner wrapper with recipe CRUD, preview serialization (PIL images, decimals to JSON-safe), and the JobManager facade | [service.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/service.py), [__init__.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/__init__.py), [jsonable.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jsonable.py) | none | Missing | TS has no synthetic data generation; implementing it would require either embedding a Python sidecar or a Swift-native generator, both large new surfaces with no current analog. |
| [Recipe generation job manager](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/manager.py): stage-based synthetic data jobs (create, dag, healthcheck, sampling, generating) with SSE event subscriptions, cancel, status and paged dataset reads via duckdb | [manager.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/manager.py), [types.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/types.py), [constants.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/constants.py), [parse.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/parse.py) | [DownloadManagerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Components/DownloadManagerView.swift) | Partial | TS streams download progress in a corner panel with persisted history, matching the presentation half, but has no multi-stage pipeline, event subscription or cancel semantics; the concrete improvement is adding named stages plus parsed progress events to the download job model. |
| [Recipe generation worker](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/worker.py): subprocess entry point executing recipe stages in a clean interpreter, emitting structured events back to the manager | [worker.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/worker.py), [__init__.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/jobs/__init__.py) | none | Missing | TS runs all work in-process (downloads, repack calls); an equivalent would be Process-spawned helper execution with stdout/queue event framing, needed for any heavy generation work. |
| [Generated code validation](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/local_callable_validators.py): validates user-supplied local callables in recipes (sandboxed subprocess checks, deep-copy isolation) and the oxc wrapper that syntax/lint checks generated JS with orphan-proof child teardown | [local_callable_validators.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/local_callable_validators.py), [validate.mjs](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/oxc-validator/validate.mjs) | none | Missing | TS executes no user code; its nearest cultural analog is the permission-gated tool runtime (Tools/Core/CommandGate.swift), and implementing recipe-code validation would ride on that same gate infrastructure. |
| [Recipe dataset export](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/export.py): bundles generated dataset artifacts into downloadable zip files with base64/preview payloads | [export.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/export.py) | [AppChatExport.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppChatExport.swift) | Partial | TS exports chats as Markdown or JSON through NSSavePanel with ghost-chat refusal, a working export pipeline for conversations only; the gap is dataset/model artifact export, and the improvement is extending AppChatExport's save-panel pattern to bundle arbitrary job outputs. |
| [HF Hub dataset push/pull](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/huggingface.py): uploads recipe datasets to Hugging Face with the account token and pulls public datasets into the recipe workspace, tagging the Data Designer footer | [huggingface.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/data_recipe/huggingface.py) | none | Missing | TS talks to the HF Hub only for model downloads; dataset upload would need write-token handling and repo creation in Swift, plus a policy decision about which profiles may publish. |
| [Model export backend](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/export/export.py): exports fine-tuned checkpoints to GGUF (pinned llama.cpp converter, imatrix, generated model card), merged 16/4-bit safetensors, MLX formats and LoRA adapters with metadata bookkeeping | [export.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/export/export.py), [__init__.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/export/__init__.py) | [repack.rs](../crates/repack/src/repack.rs) | Partial | TS's repack pipeline converts downloaded HF/GGUF checkpoints into .gturbo packs with verification (repack.rs, gturbo_writer/, hf_checkpoint), which is format conversion but intake-only; the gap is exporting to GGUF/merged formats and from trained adapters, and the improvement is adding export targets to the repack orchestrator surfaced from InstalledModelActionBarView. |
| [Export subprocess orchestrator](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/export/orchestrator.py): same API as the export backend but delegates to a persistent spawn subprocess, recycling it when transformers versions change, with account-scoped job control | [orchestrator.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/export/orchestrator.py), [worker.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/export/worker.py) | none | Missing | TS's repack runs in-process during install, which is viable because conversion is one-shot; a persistent export worker only becomes necessary once multiple export formats with heavy ML dependencies exist. |
| [Library of trained and exported models](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/library.py): unified listing of every Studio file source, including virtual model:<training|exported> entries pointing at outputs/ and exports/ directories opened in chat | [library.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/library.py) | [ModelManagerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelManagerView.swift) | Partial | TS's ModelManagerView organizes, inspects and manages downloaded models with detail panes, the same UX shape minus any trained/exported sections; the improvement is a source-enum like library.py's so outputs and exports appear as first-class manager entries. |
| [Training API schemas](../../unsloth/studio/backend/models/training.py): Pydantic definitions for training requests, S3 config, job status/progress/run summaries, and the diffusion dataset and training DTOs, plus dataset/export/recipe schemas | [training.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/models/training.py), [data_recipe.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/models/data_recipe.py), [datasets.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/models/datasets.py), [export.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/models/export.py) | none | Missing | TS defines its DTOs in Swift (ModelDownload, catalog types) and has no training request/status schema; defining them is straightforward once a job engine exists, and validation-heavy fields (paths, formats) mirror what repack's manifest checks already validate in Rust. |

## B10. RAG, research, system one, picker, YouTube, tool healing

Scope: `studio/backend/core/rag` (ingestion, hybrid store, retrieval tool, conversation archive,
folder sync), `core/research` plus `research_runs.py` (deep research supervisor), `core/systemone`
(small decision checkpoints), `picker/` (model file picker service), `core/youtube_transcript.py`,
and `core/tool_healing.py`.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [RAG document ingestion pipeline](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/ingestion.py): threaded parse, chunk, embed, store pipeline with progress events, content-hash dedup, per-page PDF extraction with Tesseract OCR fallback and vision-model figure captioning, renewable multi-process job leases, and an account-retirement guard on the RAG database | [ingestion.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/ingestion.py), [parsers.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/parsers.py), [chunking.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/chunking.py), [pdf_ocr.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/pdf_ocr.py), [captioner.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/captioner.py), [job_leases.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/job_leases.py), [account_db.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/account_db.py), [__init__.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/__init__.py) | [AttachmentImporter.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Files/AttachmentImporter.swift) | Partial | TS extracts whole documents into prompt attachments with failure reporting, but nothing is chunked, embedded, or persisted for later retrieval; implementing this would involve a local embedding model, a chunk/FTS/vector store (natural home is the Rust core with a Swift-facing tool), and a background ingestion queue with progress surfaced in the Files section. |
| [Hybrid vector and lexical store with retrieval](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/store.py): unified SQLite store of relational chunks plus FTS5 lexical and sqlite-vec dense indexes with scope-keyed incremental inserts, Reciprocal Rank Fusion retrieval with dense-score floors, env-tunable chunk/top-k config, and a swappable embedder facade (sentence-transformers or bundled GGUF llama-server with CPU/GPU fallback) | [store.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/store.py), [retrieval.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/retrieval.py), [config.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/config.py), [embeddings.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/embeddings.py), [embed_llama_server.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/embed_llama_server.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve choosing an embedder that fits the Metal-first stack (GGUF embedding model via the existing engine path in crates, or a Core ML/MLX encoder), a scoped SQLite schema with FTS5 and a vector extension, RRF fusion, and index-rebuild policy on backend switches, all exposed behind an agent tool. |
| [search_knowledge_base tool and PDF locators](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/tool.py): LLM tool that resolves KB, project, and thread scopes, formats hits as chunk blocks with a parallel citation source map, plus ingest-time computation of page highlight rectangles so a retrieved chunk can be shown on its PDF page | [tool.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/tool.py), [locators.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/locators.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve a search_knowledge_base tool registered in AppToolCatalog, scope resolution over the attachment/project stores, and, for PDFs, storing page anchors so results can deep-link into FilePreviewView; the preview UI already renders attachments, so the citation surfacing seam exists. |
| [Conversation archive recall](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/conversation_archive.py): indexes turns evicted from the rolling context window into a cumulative per-thread RAG scope and retrieves them on request, so old turns stay recallable instead of being lost, with every entry point no-opping on failure | [conversation_archive.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/conversation_archive.py) | [AppChatCompaction.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppChatCompaction.swift) | Partial | TS compaction summarizes the evicted prefix, keeps rows in the transcript and ghost vault, and re-injects one summary, but evicted content is never searchable and recall fidelity degrades to whatever the summary kept; the gap is retrieval of specific evicted turns, and the concrete improvement is indexing compacted turns into a per-chat retrieval scope (see the RAG rows) with a recall reserve in the prompt budget. |
| [Linked folder sync](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/folder_sync.py): durable, sequential, lease-coordinated reconciliation of linked local folders into the RAG index with file-identity tracking, change detection, and overlap validation so re-indexes stay incremental and safe | [folder_sync.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/folder_sync.py) | [ProjectFileIndex.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Files/ProjectFileIndex.swift) | Missing | TS only lists a project root on demand for @ autocomplete (bounded walk, 20 second freshness) and never ingests folder contents; implementing would involve a persisted folder link table, hash-based change detection over the bounded scan rules it already has (FileSystemScanRules), and incremental enqueueing into the ingestion pipeline. |
| [Web page retrieval filtering for auto-read](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/web_rank.py): deep research auto-reads top search results by ingesting scraped pages into a throwaway RAG scope and folding back only the most relevant passages, so synthesis is grounded in page text without whole pages filling the context | [web_rank.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/rag/web_rank.py) | [WebFetchExecutor.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Web/WebFetchExecutor.swift) | Partial | TS fetches pages to markdown with size and timeout caps and injects them whole, which wastes context on boilerplate for long pages; the gap is relevance filtering, and the concrete improvement is routing fetched pages through a local chunk and rank pass (reusing whatever ingestion store lands) before injection, with a throwaway scope so nothing persists. |
| [Deep research run supervisor](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research_runs.py): in-process durable supervisor that runs plan, search, auto-read, audit, and report phases against the local model with persisted resumable runs, streaming events, timeouts, retries, and rate-limit handling | [research_runs.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research_runs.py) | [AppModel+GoalLoop.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+GoalLoop.swift), [SubagentRunner.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/SubagentRunner.swift) | Partial | The goal loop (stop-seam evaluator, deferral, check-ins) plus subagents with web search/fetch already give multi-step autonomous research, but there is no dedicated planner/agent/audit/report pipeline, no persisted resumable research run store, and no per-source evidence catalogs; a concrete improvement is a research goal profile that persists plan and evidence to the chat store and resumes after relaunch, reusing the existing subagent progress surfaces. |
| [Research output parsing](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/parsing.py): recovers structured plans, agent actions, research state, and synthesis audits from a small model's free-form or truncated output and validates them against what the run actually holds | [parsing.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/parsing.py) | [ChatGoal.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ChatGoal.swift) | Partial | ChatGoal already parses evaluator verdicts (met, not met, reason) out of free-form model output with its own recovery seams, but there is no plan or action JSON recovery for a research pipeline; the gap is validated structured actions, and an improvement is extracting the verdict-parsing pattern into a reusable tolerant JSON recovery helper for any loop that consumes model-structured output. |
| [Research system prompts](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/prompts.py): hardened system prompts for the planner, agent, synthesis audit, and final report calls, including a report boundary marker, evidence-as-untrusted-data rules, and website policy text | [prompts.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/prompts.py), [__init__.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/__init__.py) | none | Missing | TS assembles system prompts from AppSystemPrompt, AppSoulPrompt, and MemoryPromptBuilder but has no research-specific prompt set; implementing would involve adding research phase prompts to the existing prompt assembly path with the same injection-hardening wording and a report boundary convention the report renderer can split on. |
| [Citation validation and repair](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/citations.py): rewrites report citations so every reference resolves to sources actually gathered, strips invented URLs and renumbered or unsupported citations, and removes model-appended source lists | [citations.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/citations.py) | none | Missing | Implementing would involve tracking gathered sources per run (search results and fetched pages already flow through WebSearchExecutor and WebFetchExecutor), then a markdown pass that canonicalizes links and numbered citations against that catalog before a research report renders; the artifact panel is a natural place to show per-claim source links. |
| [Query and evidence redaction](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/redaction.py): strips credentials, personal identifiers, and private network addresses from outgoing web queries, escapes prompt-delimiter tags in gathered evidence, and shields the report boundary from untrusted content | [redaction.py](../../unsloth/studio/backend/core/research/redaction.py) | [WebSearchExecutor.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Web/WebSearchExecutor.swift), [HttpRequestDestinationValidator.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Tools/Web/HttpRequestDestinationValidator.swift) | Partial | TS already drops URLs with embedded credentials, blocks private/loopback/metadata hosts, and applies domain gating, but there is no scrubbing of secrets or PII out of the search query text itself and no escaping of gathered evidence against prompt-delimiter injection; the concrete improvement is a query sanitizer in WebSearchExecutor and an evidence-shielding wrapper wherever fetched content is folded into prompts. |

## B11. Hub service, models, utils, integrations, plugins

Scope: `studio/backend/hub` (local inventory, downloads, datasets, tokens, endpoint proxy and
ModelScope mirror), `backend/utils` (hardware, inference defaults, drafters, paths, prebuilts,
security gates), `backend/integrations/blender`, and the two data-designer seed plugins.

## B12. Backend assets

Scope: `studio/backend/assets`: vendored chat-template overrides, fine-tune preset YAMLs, per-family
and per-model default configs, bundled sample dataset, vendored API docs UI, and the checkpoint
preview page.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Vendored Gemma 4 chat templates](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/chat_templates/gemma-4.jinja): corrected chat templates (preserve_thinking default, null-render fixes, edge-variant differences) applied to gemma-4 GGUF models so a broken embedded template never needs re-downloading | [gemma-4.jinja](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/chat_templates/gemma-4.jinja), [gemma-4-edge.jinja](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/chat_templates/gemma-4-edge.jinja) | [entry.rs](../crates/catalog/src/entry.rs), [jinja_chat_template.rs](../crates/tokenizer/src/jinja_chat_template.rs) | Partial | TS renders each model's own chat_template.jinja sidecar (the sidecar file list is per repo in entry.rs) through its own Jinja engine and ships no corrected per-family overrides, so a GGUF embedding a bad template renders badly. Improvement: bundle family-keyed override templates that are applied after install, mirroring the unsloth vendoring trick. |
| [Fine-tune preset configs](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/full_finetune.yaml): three ready-to-run training presets (full finetune, text LoRA, vision LoRA) that prefill the studio training form with sane model, data, LoRA, and logging values | [full_finetune.yaml](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/full_finetune.yaml), [lora_text.yaml](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/lora_text.yaml), [vision_lora.yaml](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/vision_lora.yaml) | none | Missing | TS is inference only, with no trainer anywhere in the Swift app or crates. Implementing parity means a fine-tuning path (adapter training over crates/runtime forward passes plus Metal kernels for backward passes), config parsing, and an output/checkpoint store before any preset YAML could be meaningful. |
| [Per-family inference defaults](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/inference_defaults.json): longest-match-first table mapping model families (qwen3, gemma, moss-tts, etc.) to curated sampling defaults (temperature, top_p, top_k, min_p, penalties) sourced from Ollama params and studio YAMLs | [inference_defaults.json](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/inference_defaults.json) | [AppSamplingSettings.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppSamplingSettings.swift), [AppRuntimeOptions.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppRuntimeOptions.swift) | Partial | TS has one global default snapshot in AppSamplingSettings (temperature 0.2, topK 64, topP 0.95, maxNewTokens 2048) plus per-model reasoning defaults, but no per-model-family sampling table. Improvement: add a sampling defaults block to crates/catalog/src/models.json entries and apply it when a model is selected, keeping the user override on top. |
| [Per-model defaults table](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/model_defaults/default.yaml): default.yaml plus about 78 per-model YAMLs under 13 family dirs carrying training hyperparameters (lr, epochs, batch, grad accumulation, optimizer, scheduler), LoRA rank/alpha/target modules, logging, and an inference block per checkpoint | [default.yaml](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/model_defaults/default.yaml), [gemma-4-26B example](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/model_defaults/gemma/unsloth_gemma-4-26B-A4B-it.yaml), [embedding example](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/configs/model_defaults/embedding/unsloth_Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B.yaml) | [models.json](../crates/catalog/src/models.json) | Partial | models.json is the per-model analog but carries provenance instead of defaults: pinned source revision, sidecar file list, download bytes, gate targets, and measured numbers. Improvement: extend catalog entries with default sampling/context knobs derived from the verified runs so a fresh install starts with tuned settings. |
| [Bundled sample dataset](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/datasets/alpaca_unsloth.json): 257-row instruction/input/output dataset bundled so a first fine-tune runs with zero external downloads | [alpaca_unsloth.json](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/datasets/alpaca_unsloth.json) | [app-prompts.json](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Resources/app-prompts.json) | Missing | TS ships sample content for chat instead (app-prompts.json driving PromptExamplesView.swift and greetings.json for greetings), not training data. A bundled dataset would only matter after a trainer exists; then a small bundled fixture dataset plus a dataset-format auto-detector like format_type auto would be the shape to copy. |
| [Vendored API docs UI](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/docs_ui/docs_ui_manifest.json): pinned swagger-ui and redoc bundles plus a sha256 manifest, served same-origin on FastAPI's /docs paths so no CDN JavaScript can read session tokens | [docs_ui_manifest.json](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/docs_ui/docs_ui_manifest.json), [README.md](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/docs_ui/README.md) | [ServerEndpointCatalog.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Server/ServerEndpointCatalog.swift) | Partial | TS documents every implemented route in-app (OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, and service families with curl snippets), test-asserted against turbospark_server's router, but there is no OpenAPI schema and no docs page served over HTTP (grep for openapi/swagger in swift and crates/server finds nothing). Improvement: generate an OpenAPI spec from the router and serve it from the local server, vendoring the UI assets the same digest-checked way. |
| [Checkpoint preview page](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/preview_page.html): self-contained CSP-hardened HTML served per training run and checkpoint with a capability token, giving a shareable public chat playground for a trained model | [preview_page.html](../../unsloth/studio/backend/assets/preview_page.html) | [AppChatShareDocument.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppChatShareDocument.swift) | Missing | The closest TS feature is chat export, which writes files locally rather than hosting a token-gated page, and TS has no training runs or checkpoints to preview. Implementing it would involve the local server serving a sandboxed standalone page bound to a loaded model plus a share token, which is only compelling if checkpoint-based sharing becomes a TS use case. |

## B13. Frontend shell and shared infrastructure

Scope: `studio/frontend` infrastructure: app shell and router, state stores, hooks, lib, config,
types, i18n, shared components (assistant-ui primitives, layout, markdown, resource-picker, tauri
bridges, ui kit, update, web), assets, build tooling, and test helpers. Feature directories under
`src/features` are covered in B14 to B16.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [State store architecture](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/stores/index.ts): placeholder global store module while real state lives in ~96 feature-scoped zustand stores (training config, keyboard shortcuts, appearance, chat runtime) | [stores/index.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/stores/index.ts), [keyboard-shortcuts-store.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/settings/stores/keyboard-shortcuts-store.ts) | [AppModel.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel.swift) | Partial | Swift app inverts the model: one @MainActor ObservableObject AppModel with ~70 extension files (AppModel+Chat, +Generation, +Persistence) instead of many small stores; both use external observation (useSyncExternalStore vs Combine @Published). |
| [App shell, routing and window lifecycle](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/app/router.tsx): TanStack Router tree of 17 routes plus window size bounds, first-launch sizing, and a desktop-shell-ready gate that swaps install/startup screens for the app | [router.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/app/router.tsx), [window-layout.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/app/window-layout.ts) | [RootView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/App/RootView.swift) | Partial | Swift has no URL router; navigation is an AppNavigationSection enum rendered by RootView (rail, top bar, panes, status strip). Window minimum size is MainWindowMinimumSize.swift and there is no multi-page route table by design. |
| [Native menu and keyboard shortcut wiring](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/app/app-menu-chords.ts): typed AppMenuAction enum bridging the Tauri File/View/Go/Help menus to settings tabs, chats and help, with parsed binding chords | [app-menu-chords.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/app/app-menu-chords.ts), [use-app-menu-actions.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/app/use-app-menu-actions.ts) | [KeyboardShortcutCatalog.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/App/KeyboardShortcutCatalog.swift) | Full | Swift ships a value-type shortcut catalog plus AlternateShortcutBridge so the Keyboard Shortcuts settings pane and tests hold the same bindings the menus dispatch. |
| [Custom window titlebar](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/tauri/window-titlebar.tsx): hand-drawn titlebar with window controls, 8-direction resize handles and sidebar pin logic for win/linux where the native frame is replaced | [window-titlebar.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/tauri/window-titlebar.tsx) | none | Missing | TurboSpark uses the native macOS NSWindow titlebar and traffic lights; in-window chrome is TopBarView (toggles, live machine load) rather than a replacement frame. |
| [Design tokens and theming](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/index.css): 4200-line token sheet with ~960 CSS custom properties, oklch palette, dark custom variant and a bridge that mirrors the appearance store onto html and the Tauri window theme | [index.css](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/index.css) | [AppearanceStore.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Theme/AppearanceStore.swift) | Full | Swift equivalent is an AppearanceArchive persisted to appearance.json (one-way migrated off UserDefaults) resolved into ResolvedAppTheme and injected via the .appTheme environment instead of CSS variables. |
| [Theme gallery and appearance customization](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/settings/stores/appearance-custom-store.ts): zustand store for custom colors plus an animated theme toggle; users restyle accent and surfaces per light/dark mode | [appearance-custom-store.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/settings/stores/appearance-custom-store.ts), [animated-theme-toggler.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/ui/animated-theme-toggler.tsx) | [ThemeGalleryView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Components/ThemeGalleryView.swift) | Full | Swift goes further: ThemeLibrary persists saved themes, ThemeGalleryView previews them, and AppDockIconRenderer even re-renders the dock icon per appearance preset; unsloth has no equivalent gallery surface. |
| [Typography, fonts and mascot assets](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/mascot-img.tsx): self-hosted variable fonts (Figtree, Space Grotesk, Inter) imported through fontsource with font-heading/font-sans roles, plus bundled mascot fallback art | [mascot-img.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/mascot-img.tsx), [mascot-fallback.webp](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/assets/mascot-fallback.webp) | [AppFontCatalog.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Theme/AppFontCatalog.swift) | Full | Swift replaces web font imports with AppFontCatalog (one role table for every themed text size), AppFontRegistrar, ThemedFont and a user-facing AppTextSize scale; mascot/character art lives in Resources (WelcomeCharacter.png etc). |
| [i18n localization](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/i18n/locale-store.ts): homegrown store with typed translation keys, lazy locale loading, timeouts, and 12 locales (ar, de, en, es, fr, hi, it, ja, ko, pt-br, ru, zh-CN) checked by a parity script | [locale-store.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/i18n/locale-store.ts), [check-parity.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/i18n/check-parity.ts) | [AppLanguage.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Theme/AppLanguage.swift) | Full | Swift covers 21 locales (superset: adds nl, pl, tr, uk, sv, vi, id, he, zh-Hant) via AppLanguage plus 21 .lproj resource folders and a 176k-line Localizable.xcstrings, with RTL handling for Arabic and Hebrew. |
| [Streaming markdown rendering](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/markdown/markdown-preview.tsx): Streamdown-based renderer with lazy code, math (KaTeX) and mermaid plugins, sandboxed images, safe links and idle-scheduled block mounting | [markdown-preview.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/markdown/markdown-preview.tsx), [markdown-plugins.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/lib/markdown-plugins.ts) | [ResponseMarkdownRenderer.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Presentation/ResponseMarkdownRenderer.swift) | Partial | Swift renders markdown into styled NSAttributedString via a custom block parser (heading, code, quote, lists) with a plain-text fallback, wired into ChatMessageMarkdownView; no KaTeX or mermaid equivalents. |
| [Code syntax highlighting](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/assistant-ui/code-themes.ts): worker-based highlighter loading only needed grammars, paired custom unsloth light/dark code themes, fence defer/windowing modes to avoid flicker on long output | [code-themes.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/assistant-ui/code-themes.ts), [reasoning-highlight.worker.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/assistant-ui/reasoning-highlight.worker.ts) | [CodeSyntaxHighlighter.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Presentation/CodeSyntaxHighlighter.swift) | Partial | Swift highlights fences natively in CodeSyntaxHighlighter for ChatMessageMarkdownView; no web-worker model, no themeable grammar packs, and language coverage is narrower. |
| [Interactive markdown tables](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/markdown/markdown-preview.tsx): tables render through the streamdown pipeline with styling only, no sort or selection affordances | [markdown-preview.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/markdown/markdown-preview.tsx) | [InteractiveMarkdownTableView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/InteractiveMarkdownTableView.swift) | Full | Swift upgrades each assistant-message pipe table to an interactive grid (MarkdownTableGrid: sorting, row selection, copy, export) while leaving prose to the ordinary renderer, exceeding the web behavior. |
| [Find-in-page](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/find-in-page/components/find-bar.tsx): DOM-walking find bar with portal attribute marking, match counting and scroll-to-highlight across the whole app surface | [find-bar.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/find-in-page/components/find-bar.tsx), [find-dom.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/find-in-page/lib/find-dom.ts) | [ChatSearchOverlayView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/ChatSearchOverlayView.swift) | Partial | Swift's Cmd+K overlay (ChatSearch matching/snippets over the in-memory chat archive) searches prior chats by title and content, not live within-transcript text find; no DOM equivalent exists in SwiftUI. |
| [Desktop update UX](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/update/release-notes-panel.tsx): animated update banner with status/failure states, dedicated update screen, release notes and llama-update changelog panels fed by a Tauri updater controller | [release-notes-panel.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/components/update/release-notes-panel.tsx), [use-tauri-update.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/hooks/use-tauri-update.ts) | [SparkleUpdateController.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/App/Updates/SparkleUpdateController.swift) | Partial | Swift delegates to Sparkle 2 with an EdDSA-signed GitHub Releases appcast and a settings auto-check toggle; there is no in-app banner, update screen, or embedded release-notes panel. |

## B14. Frontend chat feature and api-monitor

Scope: `studio/frontend/src/features/chat` (composer, transcript, sidebar, settings, adapters,
stores) and `features/api-monitor`. This is the largest single user-facing surface of the product.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Composer mode pills](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/chat/shared-composer.tsx): toggle web search, web fetch, RAG, image generation, MCP, and deep research as capability-gated pills on the composer | [shared-composer.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/chat/shared-composer.tsx), [deep-research-composer-button.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/chat/components/deep-research-composer-button.tsx), [research-activity-panel.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/chat/components/research-activity-panel.tsx), [research.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/chat/types/research.ts) | [PromptComposerPlusMenu.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/PromptComposerPlusMenu.swift) | Partial | TS plus menu covers attach, slash, MCP, skills, project switch, search, and permission toggles, but has no provider-gated web fetch, image generation, RAG, or deep research pills; gate plus-menu toggles on model capability like unsloth does. |
| [Skill @-mentions](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/chat/shared-composer.tsx): typing @ in the composer pops a skill mention picker that inserts a skill reference | [shared-composer.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/chat/shared-composer.tsx) | [ComposerAutocompleteEngine.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/ComposerAutocompleteEngine.swift), [ComposerSuggestionPopup.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Generation/ComposerSuggestionPopup.swift) | Full | TS also suggests files, folders, and typed references like @diff, @staged, @git:, @url:, so its mention surface is broader than unsloth's skill-only mentions. |

## B15. Frontend model and media views

Scope: `studio/frontend/src/features` hub, library, model-picker, loaded-models, train-model-picker,
generation-presets, dataset-picker, images, video, audio, and rag views.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Hub discover feed and search](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/hub-page.tsx): live Hugging Face feed with trending carousels, sorted search, dataset mode, format and capability filters, owner scope, and a fit-on-device toggle scored against the VRAM budget | [models-catalog.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/catalog/models-catalog.tsx), [models-toolbar.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/catalog/models-toolbar.tsx) | [ModelHubView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelHubView.swift), [ModelHubFilterBarView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelHubFilterBarView.swift) | Partial | TS has Recommended/Discover/On Device tabs with text search, format/capability/fit menus, and fit-sorted recommendations over a bundled catalog, but no live HF feed or server-side search, no dataset mode, no owner scope toggle, and no fit-on-device filter tied to a persisted VRAM budget; wiring the existing catalog against HF search endpoints plus a datasets tab would close most of the gap. |
| [Hub detail inspector and downloads](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/catalog/hub-detail-view.tsx): split-view model page with README rendering, GGUF variant and safetensors download cards, runtime status, and run handoff to chat or media pages | [gguf-download-card.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/catalog/gguf-download-card.tsx), [model-readme.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/catalog/model-readme.tsx) | [ModelDetailPaneView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelDetailPaneView.swift), [ModelInstallCards.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelInstallCards.swift) | Partial | TS detail pane covers specs, memory and hardware fit cards, install progress, and Load and Chat, and ModelProbeSheet can probe any HF repo, but there is no README view, no GGUF quant variant selection with per-file download cards, and no run handoff into a media page; a variant fetch plus a markdown renderer would close it. |
| [Hub download manager](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/download-manager/download-manager-panel.tsx): managed download queue with staged downloads, progress reconciliation, cancel, transport preference and conflict handling, and start toasts | [download-manager-controller.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/download-manager/download-manager-controller.ts), [transport-conflict.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/download-manager/transport-conflict.ts) | [ModelInstallCards.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelInstallCards.swift), [DownloadETAEstimator.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/DownloadETAEstimator.swift) | Partial | TS shows one active install card with bytes, fraction, ETA, and cancel, but no queue panel for concurrent jobs, no staged or adopted downloads, and no transport conflict dialog; a queue view over the existing ModelDownload state would be the concrete improvement. |
| [On-device inventory](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/inventory/use-device-inventory.ts): unified list of cached and local models and datasets with type filter, sort by recent/name/size, manage-folders dialog, and a free-up-space workflow | [on-device-folders-dialog.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/catalog/on-device-folders-dialog.tsx), [free-up-space-dialog.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/hub/catalog/free-up-space-dialog.tsx) | [ModelManagerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelManagerView.swift), [ModelManagerFilters.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelManagerFilters.swift) | Partial | TS Model Manager searches, filters, sorts, reveals, and deletes installed models with organization metadata and a location picker, but there is no dataset inventory, no manage-folders dialog, and no disk reclamation pass; the free-up-space dialog is the most valuable missing piece. |
| [Per-model config page](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/model-picker/components/model-config-page.tsx): per-model load settings with context length checkpoints, memory estimate row, chat template editor, resident memory request, and raw llama.cpp extra args | [per-model-config.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/model-picker/model-config/per-model-config.ts), [memory-fit.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/model-picker/model-config/memory-fit.ts) | [ContextLadderView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ContextLadderView.swift), [InstalledModelMemoryFitCardView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/InstalledModelMemoryFitCardView.swift) | Partial | TS shows a memory-by-context ladder and fit verdicts but has no per-model persisted load config, no context length override, no chat template editor, and no extra-args editor; unsloth only reaches control vectors through raw --control-vector flags while TS ships a real steering card, so the gap is config persistence and the context/template controls. |
| [Shared model selector](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/model-picker/components/model-selector.tsx): one reusable picker used by chat, images, and video with catalog grouping, quant labels, per-host artifact policy, and remembered per-page choices | [sidebar-model-config.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/model-picker/components/sidebar-model-config.tsx), [model-config-page.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/model-picker/components/model-config-page.tsx) | [ModelRecommendationRow.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelRecommendationRow.swift) | Partial | TS picks models through hub and manager rows with recommendation probes and fit verdicts rather than a shared picker component, so media pages cannot reuse one selection surface with per-host quant policy; extracting a shared picker would also serve a future media routing story. |
| [Loaded models indicator](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/loaded-models/loaded-models-indicator.tsx): draggable floating card listing every resident model across text, tts, image, video, and stt runtimes with kind icons and in-place eject | [use-loaded-models.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/loaded-models/use-loaded-models.ts), [use-drag-position.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/loaded-models/use-drag-position.ts) | [InstalledModelActionBarView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/InstalledModelActionBarView.swift) | Partial | TS unloads from the manager action bar and the hub flags a resident row, but no cross-page surface lists everything holding memory at once; a compact resident-models popover in the app chrome over the existing load state would close it. |

| [Dataset picker](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/dataset-picker/components/dataset-selector.tsx): pick training datasets from HF search, previously downloaded repos, or local files with exact-id commit validation | [dataset-selector-lists.tsx](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/dataset-picker/components/dataset-selector-lists.tsx) | none | Missing | Requires a training pipeline that consumes datasets plus a dataset scanner and cache; without training there is no consumer, so this waits on the train-model-picker work. |

| [Images studio](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/images/images-page.tsx): image generation page with workflow tabs (create, transform, inpaint, extend, upscale, reference, edit), negative prompt, dense-quant model options, and a resizable gallery rail | [workflows.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/images/workflows.ts), [image-generation-defaults.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/images/image-generation-defaults.ts) | [ImagesSectionView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImagesSectionView.swift), [ImageComposerView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageComposerView.swift), [ImageGenerationSettings.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Images/ImageGenerationSettings.swift) | Partial | TS covers prompt entry, model download offers with a recommendation sheet, size, seed, batch of 1-4 via ImageGenerationSequence, gallery with multi-select delete, export, and Finder reveal, but has no workflow tabs, no negative prompt, and no inpaint or upscale paths. |

## B16. Frontend training, recipes, export, settings, auth, and misc features

Scope: the remaining `studio/frontend/src/features` dirs: training, data-recipes, recipe-studio,
export, settings, auth, security, profile, credentials, hf-auth, npu, igpu-carveout, studio, tour,
deep-links, native-intents, and transformers-upgrade (find-in-page and api-monitor are in B13/B14).

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Training run configuration](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/training/stores/training-config-store.ts): pick model and dataset, choose LoRA/QLoRA methods with device support gates, edit hyperparameters, and validate or serialize the YAML config before a run | [training/index.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/training/index.ts), [training-methods.ts](../../unsloth/studio/frontend/src/features/training/lib/training-methods.ts) | none | Missing | TurboSpark is inference-only Metal decode with no fine-tuning path anywhere in the Swift app, so there is no config store or method gate to compare. |
