## B17. Studio installers and docs

Scope: the studio-level engine installers, python stack installer, setup entry points, pin files,
the Colab notebook, package.json, and studio/scripts (MCP.md, PDF_OCR.md, NVLINK_P2P.md and
ROCM_RDNA2_APU.md are covered in section B2).

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Chat, diffusion, and speech engine installers](../../unsloth/studio/install_llama_prebuilt.py): cross-platform downloaders that fetch pinned llama-server, sd-cli (stable-diffusion.cpp), and whisper-server prebuilts into an isolated home with checksum verification, per-host asset resolution, and swap-into-place installs | [install_llama_prebuilt.py](../../unsloth/studio/install_llama_prebuilt.py), [install_sd_cpp_prebuilt.py](../../unsloth/studio/install_sd_cpp_prebuilt.py), [install_whisper_prebuilt.py](../../unsloth/studio/install_whisper_prebuilt.py) | [swift-lib.sh](../scripts/swift-lib.sh) | Missing | TS needs no such provisioner by design: the Metal engine is a statically linked crates/ffi library copied into the SwiftPM package by swift-lib.sh, so there is no external engine binary. The capability becomes relevant only if TS adds engines it does not compile (for example a CPU fallback), at which point the pinned-checksum installer pattern is the one to copy. |
| [NPU and Node runtime installers](../../unsloth/studio/install_lemonade_prebuilt.py): provision pinned Lemonade/FastFlowLM binaries for NPU support, Microsoft WXC executables, and an isolated Node.js 24 LTS runtime that never touches system Node | [install_lemonade_prebuilt.py](../../unsloth/studio/install_lemonade_prebuilt.py), [install_mxc_prebuilt.py](../../unsloth/studio/install_mxc_prebuilt.py), [install_node_prebuilt.py](../../unsloth/studio/install_node_prebuilt.py) | none | Missing | TS has no NPU or Windows path (Metal on Apple Silicon only) and no web frontend toolchain, so none of these runtimes exist in the app. Implementing any would start with an accelerator abstraction that the ArchConfig family dispatch could select, which the current compile-in engine deliberately avoids. |
| [Python dependency stack installer](../../unsloth/studio/install_python_stack.py): large cross-platform pip orchestrator that builds the studio venv (torch wheel selection, flash-attn and xformers wheels, staged replacement installs, platform allowlists) after setup creates it | [install_python_stack.py](../../unsloth/studio/install_python_stack.py) | none | Missing | TS ships no Python at runtime; the app is Swift plus one Rust staticlib and depends only on system frameworks. There is nothing sensible to implement unless a Python-driven feature is added, and the honest parity note is that TS eliminated this whole failure surface by not having an interpreter dependency. |
| [Studio setup entry points](../../unsloth/studio/setup.sh): setup.sh, setup.ps1, and setup.bat drive full environment bring-up (venv, managed Node, frontend build, engine installs); the bat is a PowerShell execution-policy and mark-of-the-web shim | [setup.sh](../../unsloth/studio/setup.sh), [setup.ps1](../../unsloth/studio/setup.ps1), [setup.bat](../../unsloth/studio/setup.bat) | [swift-lib.sh](../scripts/swift-lib.sh), [ModelDownload.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ModelDownload.swift) | Partial | TS has no end-user setup script because the app ships as a self-contained bundle; developer builds run through Make targets (swift-lib, swift-test, swift-app) with scripts/swift-lib.sh building the FFI staticlib, and first-run onboarding happens in-app via the Installation model hub and the ModelDownload queue. Gap: none to close for a signed app download, but a doctor-style self-check script for developer checkout failures would be a concrete improvement. |
| [Prebuilt pin files](../../unsloth/studio/lemonade_prebuilt_pins.json): committed sha256 trust anchors (schema versioned) for lemonade, fastflowlm, and Node archives so installers verify against in-tree digests rather than the download origin | [lemonade_prebuilt_pins.json](../../unsloth/studio/lemonade_prebuilt_pins.json), [node_prebuilt_pins.json](../../unsloth/studio/node_prebuilt_pins.json) | [entry.rs](../crates/catalog/src/entry.rs) | Partial | TS pins what it fetches rather than binaries: catalog rows pin source repo revisions (commit sha where the publisher offers one, with download_bytes as a fingerprint test) and the committed Cargo.lock pins Rust dependency versions. Gap: no digest trust anchors exist because no external archives are downloaded; if any provisioner is added it should ship a pins file of exactly this shape. |
| [Colab notebook](../../unsloth/studio/Unsloth_Studio_Colab.ipynb): eight-cell notebook that on a free Colab T4 clones the repo, runs studio/setup.sh --local, starts the backend via colab.start() which opens a Cloudflare tunnel and prints admin login credentials, giving a zero-install browser tour of Studio | [Unsloth_Studio_Colab.ipynb](../../unsloth/studio/Unsloth_Studio_Colab.ipynb) | none | Missing | TS requires a local Apple Silicon Mac with Metal, so there is no cloud or browser-hosted path at all. Implementing parity would need a GPU-hosted build of the engine plus a remote-session UI and token handoff, which conflicts with the current local-first architecture and is not worth pursuing for parity's sake. |
| [Tauri CLI pin manifest](../../unsloth/studio/package.json): not a real package; a lockfile holder pinning @tauri-apps/cli 2.10.1 so the desktop release workflow resolves a reproducible packaging CLI via npm ci | [package.json](../../unsloth/studio/package.json) | none | Missing | TS has no npm layer at all: release packaging is Make driven (swift-app, app-bundle, dmg per docs/RELEASE.md) and the pinned-toolchain role is played by the committed Cargo.lock plus the platforms line kept in sync in scripts/swift-lib.sh. Nothing to implement; the parity observation is that TS avoids a whole package manager by not shipping a web frontend. |
| [Studio scripts directory](../../unsloth/studio/scripts/unsloth_freeze_report.py): a diagnostics harness that relaunches the app once per candidate workaround, records whether the interface stayed up, and emits a machine-described freeze report users can attach to bugs | [unsloth_freeze_report.py](../../unsloth/studio/scripts/unsloth_freeze_report.py) | [AppDiagnostics.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppDiagnostics.swift) | Partial | TS collects diagnostics in-app (AppDiagnostics.swift and the Diagnostics inspector sections for sampling, runner, telemetry, and usage) but has no scriptable repro harness that exercises the app repeatedly and packages evidence. Improvement: add a small script or in-app command that captures app version, model, ArchConfig family, and recent diagnostics into one shareable report for bug reports. |

## B18. unsloth package part 1: model loading and family coverage

Scope: the `unsloth/` package model side: loader and loader utils, family training patches (llama,
qwen, gemma, mistral, niche families), registry, vision, RL, DPO, diffusion, and sentence-transformer
modules. Framing: these are capabilities the Unsloth Desktop app inherits from the training library.
TurboSpark is inference-only, so training rows are Missing and family rows compare against the
inference-side family dispatch.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [One-command model load into a chat session](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/loader.py): the app loads an installed local model, attaches it to a conversation, and starts streaming decode | [loader.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/loader.py), [__init__.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/__init__.py) | [AppModel+Models.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+Models.swift), [ModelLoaderControl.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Chrome/ModelLoaderControl.swift) | Partial | TS loads resident quantized checkpoints and decodes chat; unsloth's path is HF/transformers load plus 4bit QLoRA and PEFT attach, which TS has none of. |
| [Automatic device and memory placement](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/loader_utils.py): planner picks where model layers live so a chosen checkpoint fits the machine | [loader_utils.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/loader_utils.py) | [InstalledModelMemoryFitCardView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/InstalledModelMemoryFitCardView.swift), [load_guard.rs](../crates/model-io/src/load_guard.rs) | Partial | TS plans memory fit and guards loads against overcommit for inference; unsloth's device_map planner, offload hints, and fp8 mode selection are training-load concerns TS does not implement. |
| [Checkpoint name and layout mapping](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/mapper.py): a quantized download resolves to a runnable local artifact | [mapper.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/mapper.py) | [arch_registry.rs](../crates/repack/src/arch_registry.rs), [mod.rs](../crates/model-io/src/manifest/mod.rs) | Partial | Unsloth maps unsloth-bnb-4bit names to upstream repos for training round trips; TS's repack registry maps HF/GGUF configs to .gturbo artifacts, so the intake direction is covered. |
| [Hub access and environment plumbing](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/_utils.py): shared dtype, auth, and patching helpers behind every load | [_utils.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/_utils.py) | [HfEndpointResolution.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/HfEndpointResolution.swift) | Partial | TS resolves HF endpoints and manages downloads; the 5900-line file's bf16 checks, HF login, and transformers monkey-patch helpers have no desktop-app counterpart. |
| [Unified-memory aware weight loading](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/_uma_safetensors.py): integrated-GPU unified memory gets a pinned-load fast path | [_uma_safetensors.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/_uma_safetensors.py) | [resident_buffer.rs](../crates/model-io/src/resident_buffer.rs) | Partial | Unsloth patches safetensors loading for integrated UMA GPUs; TS is built end to end on Apple Silicon unified-memory resident buffers, which is the same idea on the inference side. |

| [Forced custom dtype override](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/_custom_dtype.py): environment variable forces a load dtype with a hardened wire format | [_custom_dtype.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/_custom_dtype.py) | none | Missing | TS has no user-facing dtype override; decode dtype is fixed by the installed artifact and ArchConfig. |

| [Llama and Mistral chat models](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/llama.py): the most common open families train and run patched for speed | [llama.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/llama.py), [mistral.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/mistral.py) | [mod.rs](../crates/runtime/src/families/llama/mod.rs), [family.rs](../crates/model-io/src/arch_config/family.rs) | Partial | Unsloth patches Llama/Mistral for fast training; TS dispatches the Llama family (one string also covers Mistral and Mixtral) for Metal decode, and never trains. |

| [Niche family coverage](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/cohere.py): Cohere, Granite, Falcon-H1, GLM-4.7-MoE, Llama 4, and Longcat trainable | [cohere.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/cohere.py), [granite.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/granite.py), [falcon_h1.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/falcon_h1.py), [glm4_moe.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/glm4_moe.py), [llama4.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/llama4.py), [longcat_lsa.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/longcat_lsa.py) | none | Missing | None of these families ship in TS; its niche coverage goes to different families (gpt-oss, DeepSeek, MiniMax, Spark, MuseGlimmer). |
| [Curated model catalog](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/registry.py): typed registry of ready-to-use quantized models per family and size | [registry.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/registry.py), [_llama.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/_llama.py), [_qwen.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/_qwen.py), [_gemma.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/_gemma.py), [_mistral.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/_mistral.py), [_phi.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/_phi.py), [_deepseek.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/registry/_deepseek.py) | [ModelHubView.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelHubView.swift), [AppModel+ModelDiscovery.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/AppModel+ModelDiscovery.swift) | Partial | The app has a browsable hub with discovery, filtering, and fit gating; it is not a static code registry of unsloth-published 4bit names, and it carries no bnb/unsloth quant tags. |

| [GRPO and other RL training](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/rl.py): policy-gradient reinforcement learning against vLLM rollouts | [rl.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/rl.py), [rl_config_compat.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/rl_config_compat.py), [rl_replacements.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/models/rl_replacements.py) | none | Missing | TS has no training of any kind, so RL trainers, config compat, and replacement tables are fully absent. |

## B19. unsloth package part 2: kernels, save, trainer, dataprep

Scope: the rest of the `unsloth/` package: Triton training kernels (norm, rope, activations, LoRA,
attention dispatch, fp8, MoE grouped GEMM), trainer and save/export, tokenizer and dataset utils,
chat-template toolbelt, dataprep, hub helpers, environment bootstrap, and QGaLore optimizers.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [Fused norm, RoPE and activation kernels](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/rms_layernorm.py): the app executes every layer with its own fused Metal RMSNorm, rotary embedding, and SwiGLU/GeGLU activation kernels for fast decode and prefill | [rms_layernorm.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/rms_layernorm.py), [rope_embedding.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/rope_embedding.py), [swiglu.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/swiglu.py), [geglu.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/geglu.py), [layernorm.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/layernorm.py) | [rms_norm.rs](../crates/gpu/src/rms_norm.rs), [utility.metal](../crates/gpu/src/shaders/utility.metal) | Partial | Unsloth ships Triton kernels that patch training forwards; TurboSpark independently implements the inference equivalents (rope.rs, activation shaders in utility.metal) and inherits none of the training patching. |
| [Cross entropy training loss](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/cross_entropy_loss.py): fused chunked cross entropy for training backward passes; the app computes no training loss at all | [cross_entropy_loss.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/cross_entropy_loss.py), [kernels/__init__.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/__init__.py), [kernels/utils.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/utils.py) | none | Missing | kernels/__init__.py is the patch facade and utils.py the shared Triton launch settings; both are training-side only. App-side logit work is inference softmax in the gpu crate. |
| [Fused LoRA kernels](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/fast_lora.py): fused LoRA MLP, QKV and O projection forward and backward for adapter training; the app has no LoRA path | [fast_lora.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/fast_lora.py) | none | Missing | TS is inference-only; no adapter compute exists in the Swift app or the runtime crates. |
| [Attention backend dispatch](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/flex_attention.py): selects sdpa/flash/xformers/flex attention backends with softcapping and sliding window masks; the app fixes attention to its own Metal kernels with no backend selection | [flex_attention.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/flex_attention.py), [attention_dispatch.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/attention_dispatch.py), [utils/__init__.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/__init__.py) | none | Missing | Inference attention lives in the gpu crate attention kernels and attention.metal shaders; utils/__init__.py is the shared helper facade both rows of this package hang off. |
| [FP8 kernels and compressed export](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/fp8.py): on-device weight compression exists in the install flow, but as intake-time per-row int4/int8 repack, not FP8 W8A8 kernels or llm-compressor FP8/NVFP4 export | [fp8.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/fp8.py), [_compressed_quantize.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/_compressed_quantize.py) | [lib.rs](../crates/repack/src/lib.rs) | Partial | _compressed_quantize.py is a standalone subprocess running llm-compressor over a merged 16-bit checkpoint; the repack crate quantizes during .gturbo install instead. |
| [MoE grouped GEMM kernels](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/moe/): grouped GEMM training kernels with a run-once autotune cache; the app runs MoE through its own Metal MoE kernels with no grouped GEMM training or autotune cache | [moe/ package](../../unsloth/unsloth/kernels/moe/) | none | Missing | grouped_gemm subpackage, autotune_cache.py and tests are one unit; inference MoE dispatch is the gpu crate moe kernels and moe.metal shaders. |
| [Trainer stack](../../unsloth/unsloth/trainer.py): SFT, DPO, GRPO and vision trainer wrappers with packing and padding-free batch logic; the app has no training loop of any kind | [trainer.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/trainer.py) | none | Missing | Closest app-side analog is conversational evaluation, not optimization. |
| [Save and export suite](../../unsloth/unsloth/save.py): export merged 16-bit checkpoints, LoRA adapters, GGUF quantizations and hub push; the app only consumes models and never exports one | [save.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/save.py) | none | Missing | GGUF appears only as a download and intake source via crates/catalog. |
| [Tokenizer patching for training](../../unsloth/unsloth/tokenizer_utils.py): loads and repairs tokenizers (BOS dedupe, backend repair, embedding resize) before training; app tokenizer handling is inference-only | [tokenizer_utils.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/tokenizer_utils.py) | none | Missing | Inference tokenization and BOS conventions live in the tokenizer crate and its dialect modules. |
| [Sequence packing and prefix grouping](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/packing.py): padding-free packed batches, hybrid packing gates, and the GRPO shared-prefix FlexAttention dedup; nothing in the app packs sequences | [packing.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/packing.py), [prefix_grouper.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/prefix_grouper.py), [prefix_grouper_kernel.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/prefix_grouper_kernel.py) | none | Missing | All three exist to make training batches dense; the app decodes one conversation stream with a KV ring instead. |
| [Chat template toolbelt](../../unsloth/unsloth/chat_templates.py): the app renders chat from each checkpoint's own Jinja template under fuel and output limits, with per-dialect fallback renderers and template-derived stop sequences | [chat_templates.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/chat_templates.py) | [jinja_chat_template.rs](../crates/tokenizer/src/jinja_chat_template.rs) | Partial | Unsloth additionally selects templates by name, repairs templates, derives Ollama EOS tokens and builds training stopping criteria; the app covers rendering only via jinja_chat_template.rs, chatml fallbacks and stop_matcher.rs. |
| [Ollama template mappers](../../unsloth/unsloth/ollama_template_mappers.py): export a model with an Ollama-named template and stop parameters; the app can index local Ollama libraries in place but maps no template names and exports nothing | [ollama_template_mappers.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/ollama_template_mappers.py) | none | Missing | Nearest parallel is Ollama as a discovered provider in State/ModelProviderDiscovery.swift, which is read-only model discovery, not template mapping. |
| [Dataprep toolkit](../../unsloth/unsloth/dataprep/__init__.py): load raw text and JSON into chunked causal datasets and synthesize Q&A corpora via a local vLLM server; the app reads documents only as chat context, never as training data | [dataprep/__init__.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/dataprep/__init__.py), [raw_text.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/dataprep/raw_text.py), [synthetic.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/dataprep/synthetic.py), [synthetic_configs.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/dataprep/synthetic_configs.py), [dataset_num_proc.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/dataset_num_proc.py) | none | Missing | File ingestion exists in the app (Presentation/DocumentTextExtractor.swift, Files/AttachmentImporter.swift) but feeds prompts, not datasets; dataset_num_proc.py is a multiprocessing heuristic for Dataset.map. |
| [HF hub helpers](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/hf_hub.py): the app resolves HF endpoints including mirror overrides and downloads full repos or single files with pause, resume and verification | [hf_hub.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/utils/hf_hub.py) | [HfEndpointResolution.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/HfEndpointResolution.swift), [ModelDownload.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/State/ModelDownload.swift) | Partial | Unsloth's file reads hub metadata such as parameter counts for training context; the app fetches via crates/catalog hf helpers but does not consume get_model_info-style metadata. |
| [Disk space checks](../../unsloth/unsloth/disk_utils.py): the app measures volume free space and gates model installs on it before starting a download | [disk_utils.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/disk_utils.py) | [ModelInstallGate.swift](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Sources/TurboSparkApp/Installation/ModelInstallGate.swift) | Partial | ModelInstallGate.freeSpace reads volumeAvailableCapacityForImportantUsageKey and feeds install gating in AppModel+Installation.swift; unsloth instead estimates GGUF export bytes and redirects Kaggle tmp during save. |
| [Device type detection](../../unsloth/unsloth/device_type.py): the app targets exactly one platform by contract (macOS Metal) and needs no CUDA, ROCm, NPU or MLX backend dispatch; runtime introspection is limited to Metal working-set and device name queries | [device_type.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/device_type.py) | [device_memory.rs](../crates/gpu/src/device_memory.rs) | Partial | device_memory.rs returns recommendedMaxWorkingSetSize and the device name for memory fit cards; there is no multi-backend detection to compare against unsloth's DEVICE_COUNT and arch workarounds. |
| [Python environment bootstrap](../../unsloth/unsloth/_auto_install.py): pip dependency auto-install, bitsandbytes native-kernel probing, import monkey fixes and version constants; not applicable to a native Swift app that ships its own engine and self-updates | [_auto_install.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/_auto_install.py), [bnb_availability.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/bnb_availability.py), [import_fixes.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/import_fixes.py), [_version.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/_version.py) | none | Missing | App update and versioning is handled by App/Updates/SparkleUpdateController.swift; no Python dependency layer exists to repair. |
| [GPU init and bf16 probing](../../unsloth/unsloth/_gpu_init.py): probes bf16 support and forces single-GPU compile workers at CUDA startup; Metal bf16 is a platform guarantee in TS so no runtime probe exists | [_gpu_init.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/_gpu_init.py) | none | Missing | bf16 execution is assumed by gpu crate kernels such as gemv_bf16.rs; an unsupported device fails at engine startup, not via capability detection. |
| [QGaLore optimizers](../../unsloth/unsloth/optimizers/__init__.py): 8-bit AdamW with a quantized GaLore projector for low-memory fine-tuning; the app performs no optimization steps | [optimizers/__init__.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/optimizers/__init__.py), [q_galore_adamw.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/optimizers/q_galore_adamw.py), [q_galore_projector.py](../../unsloth/unsloth/optimizers/q_galore_projector.py) | none | Missing | Pure training-side capability with no inference counterpart anywhere in the app or crates. |

## B20. Repo root: CLI, scripts, docker, tests, CI, installers, licensing

Scope: repo-root material of the unsloth checkout: the `unsloth` CLI, packaging and build scripts,
the curl installers, repo hygiene, README and governance docs, licensing, scripts/ and docker/
collections, tests/, and .github/ CI.

| Feature | Unsloth source | TurboSpark source | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [CLI command surface](../../unsloth/cli.py): `unsloth` console script wrapping typer app with train, chat, inference, export, start, and studio subcommands plus a dedicated `unsloth_cli/tests/` suite | [cli.py](../../unsloth/cli.py), [unsloth_cli/](../../unsloth/unsloth_cli/) | [main.rs](../crates/cli/src/main.rs), [chat.rs](../crates/cli/src/chat.rs) | Partial | TS cli is the turbospark-check binary plus --prompt/--messages-file/--chat REPL, agent.rs, daemon.rs, and bin/ subcommands; no train, export, or studio-launch subcommands. |
| [Starter fine-tune script](../../unsloth/unsloth-cli.py): standalone argparse example that loads a model, applies PEFT, trains, and saves or pushes | [unsloth-cli.py](../../unsloth/unsloth-cli.py) | none | Missing | The Swift app is inference and chat focused; there is no scripted training path anywhere in the TS app or workspace. |
| [Python packaging manifest](../../unsloth/pyproject.toml): setuptools config, dependency table, dynamic version, `[project.scripts] unsloth` entry point, package-data exclusions, and MANIFEST.in sdist prunes for tests, frontend, and Tauri crate trees | [pyproject.toml](../../unsloth/pyproject.toml), [MANIFEST.in](../../unsloth/MANIFEST.in) | [Makefile](../Makefile), [RELEASE.md](../docs/RELEASE.md) | Partial | TS ships via Makefile swift-app, app-bundle, and dmg targets plus decoupled on-demand crates.io publishing of the workspace crates documented in RELEASE.md; no Python packaging layer. |
| [Release build wrapper](../../unsloth/build.sh): builds the Vite frontend with Tailwind validation, stamps display-only release metadata, builds wheel/sdist, and on `publish` verifies the stamp and uploads the wheel with twine | [build.sh](../../unsloth/build.sh) | [swift-lib.sh](../scripts/swift-lib.sh), [make-app-bundle.sh](../scripts/make-app-bundle.sh), [make-dmg.sh](../scripts/make-dmg.sh) | Partial | TS splits the equivalent work across Makefile swift-lib/swift-app targets, bundle and DMG scripts, and the release.yml workflow; no single build wrapper. |
| [One-line shell installers](../../unsloth/install.sh): giant piped curl installers for the Studio runtime: POSIX install.sh (~478KB, function-wrapped so the pipe drains, uv env setup, rollback, launcher shortcuts) and PowerShell install.ps1 (~677KB, rollback, AV-shape tests) | [install.sh](../../unsloth/install.sh), [install.ps1](../../unsloth/install.ps1) | none | Missing | No host-environment bootstrap installer in TS; model acquisition happens in-app through the Installation UI and the app is distributed as a built DMG. |
| [Pre-commit hooks](../../unsloth/.pre-commit-config.yaml): ruff lint and fix, a kwarg-spacing ruff format hook, and allowScripts pin sync, driven by pre-commit.ci with monthly autoupdate | [.pre-commit-config.yaml](../../unsloth/.pre-commit-config.yaml) | [Makefile](../Makefile) | Partial | TS enforces equivalents through Makefile fmt-check, clippy, and catalog-guard targets and CI; no pre-commit framework. |
| [Repo hygiene config](../../unsloth/.gitignore): ignores for build artifacts and env files plus .gitattributes LF policy for .py and .sh and -text pinning of vendored docs-UI bundles | [.gitignore](../../unsloth/.gitignore), [.gitattributes](../../unsloth/.gitattributes) | [.gitignore](../.gitignore) | Partial | TS has a .gitignore but no .gitattributes line-ending policy file. |
| [Project README](../../unsloth/README.md): desktop app pitch with platform download links, quickstart, feature overview, and notebook links | [README.md](../../unsloth/README.md) | [README.md](../README.md) | Full | TS README covers install, quickstart, supported models, and benchmark results for the macOS app. |
| [Community docs](../../unsloth/CONTRIBUTING.md): contribution guide (issue hygiene, PR expectations) and Contributor Covenant code of conduct | [CONTRIBUTING.md](../../unsloth/CONTRIBUTING.md), [CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md](../../unsloth/CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md) | none | Missing | No contributor guide or code of conduct at the TS root. |
| [Licensing structure](../../unsloth/LICENSE): Apache-2.0 for the core library with AGPL-3.0 for the studio and CLI surface (full text in COPYING and studio/LICENSE.AGPL-3.0); every source file carries an SPDX header | [LICENSE](../../unsloth/LICENSE), [COPYING](../../unsloth/COPYING), [LICENSE.AGPL-3.0](../../unsloth/studio/LICENSE.AGPL-3.0) | [LICENSE](../LICENSE), [NOTICE](../NOTICE) | Partial | TS is single-license MIT with a NOTICE file tracking third-party MIT-derived material; no per-component dual licensing. |
| [Release and supply-chain scripts](../../unsloth/scripts): release stamping (stamp_studio_release.py), npm lockfile audits and pins (scan_npm_packages.py, lockfile_supply_chain_audit.py, sync_allow_scripts_pins.py), VirusTotal delta scanning (virustotal_scan.py, virustotal_delta.py), and uninstallers (uninstall.sh, uninstall.ps1) | [scripts/](../../unsloth/scripts/) | [make-app-bundle.sh](../scripts/make-app-bundle.sh), [make-sparkle-appcast.sh](../scripts/make-sparkle-appcast.sh), [RELEASE.md](../docs/RELEASE.md) | Partial | TS covers app bundling, DMG, and Sparkle appcast generation with documented release gates; no npm lockfile auditing or installer malware-scan tooling. |

| [Docker runtime images](../../unsloth/docker): CUDA and ROCm Dockerfiles plus a Studio image, Jupyter notebook runtime, entrypoints, launch scripts, and smoke tests for containerized training and serving | [Dockerfile](../../unsloth/docker/Dockerfile), [Dockerfile.rocm](../../unsloth/docker/Dockerfile.rocm) | none | Missing | TS is a macOS-only native app; no docker directory or container targets exist (verified at the repo root). |
| [Core regression test suite](../../unsloth/tests): roughly 260 entries at tests/ root covering model loading and mappings, quantization, LoRA and QLoRA, attention kernels, saving and export, and security, with grouped suites fast_inference/, qlora/, saving/, security/, kaggle/, notebooks/ | [tests/](../../unsloth/tests/) | [TurboSparkAppTests/](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Tests/TurboSparkAppTests/), [Makefile](../Makefile) | Partial | TS pairs 167 XCTest files exercising app, tool, and state behavior with cargo test --workspace per Makefile test-debug/test-release; scope is app-level, not ML kernel regression. |
| [Studio and installer test suites](../../unsloth/tests/studio): browser-driven UI probes (playwright helpers), appimage portability and model-download smokes, install/freeze tests, and PowerShell setup coverage in tests/studio_setup_ps1/ | [studio](../../unsloth/tests/studio), [studio_setup_ps1](../../unsloth/tests/studio_setup_ps1) | [TurboSparkAppTests/](../swift/TurboSparkApp/Tests/TurboSparkAppTests/) | Partial | TS XCTest covers UI state, security guards, and tools but has no browser-driven UI smoke matrix or installer-shape tests. |
